原油だけでなく石油精製品や天然ガスなどにも供給制約。インフレ圧力は長期化
◼ 原油高に加え、製油能力逼迫に伴うクラック・スプレッドの上昇もグローバルなインフレを助長する懸念
― クラック・スプレッド（石油精製品と原油の価格差）は例年を大きく上回る水準。戦争の影響を背景に中東・ロシアからの
輸出が減少していること等が影響
◼ 代替ルートの乏しい天然ガスや肥料（尿素など）の供給懸念は継続。先物価格は高止まり
― 天然ガスでは中東依存度が高い欧州やアジアで価格が上昇。欧州ガス価格（TTF）は前年比で約2.4倍まで急騰

― 先物価格も需給ひっ迫継続を見越して高止まり。中東情勢次第では、天然ガス価格がさらに上振れる可能性も

◼ 設備・資材需要の増加
◼ 電力・人材需要の増加
◼ 資金需要の拡大

インフレ長期化：エネルギー供給ショックや投資需要がインフレ圧力に
◼ ホルムズ海峡の封鎖長期化により、エネルギー価格は当面高止まり
― 開戦以降、グローバルCPIのエネルギー指数は前年比約15％まで上昇。原油や石化製品などの高騰が、航空運賃など
のサービス価格や、輸送コストの上昇を通じた幅広い財価格に波及
◼ 旺盛なAI関連需要が物価を押し上げ
― データセンター投資需要を受けメモリー価格が高騰。PC・スマホ等の最終製品価格へも波及
― 需要拡大に伴う電力価格の上昇も相まって、供給ショックと投資需要が同時に物価を押し上げ

（注） 政府機関閉鎖による米CPIデータ欠損のため、2025年10月は欠値
（出所） OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

高金利の常態化：政策金利に加え、タームプレミアムも金利を押し上げ
◼ 主要国・地域の中銀はインフレ警戒から利上げモードへ移行
― ホルムズ危機長期化や投資需要の強さに伴うインフレ圧力の高まりを受け、日米欧の中銀はいずれも9月に利上げ
◼ 日本以外の主要国においても、政策金利の上昇織り込みや財政懸念を背景に長期金利は上昇
― 米国では財政懸念に加え、足元では政策金利の上昇織り込みが長期金利を押し上げ

### Page 13

成長・負担の偏在が広げる「耐性格差」―4つの緊張関係
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
②企業・家計間の緊張
生産性上昇
企業収益増加
遅れる賃金
・所得への波及
③企業間の緊張
大企業
／AI・戦略分野
中小企業
／非AI・戦略分野
④家計間の緊張
資産保有層
／高所得層
資産非保有層
／中低所得層
①国家間の緊張
技術／資本／資源
を持つ国
技術／資本／資源
を持たない国
→
成長の実感乏しく、社会的受容性が低下
→
保護主義・産業政策競争が加速
→
消費・資産形成力の差が累積
→
投資余力の差が次の成長機会を左右
格差は一時的か、固定化・自己増幅するか
（耐性格差が強まる中での日本の立ち位置は？）
投資主導経済＋インフレ＋高金利 拡大する耐性格差成長・負担の偏在
偏在化する成長の果実「新常態」下の金融市場中東危機長期化、加速するAI・レジリエンス投資

### Page 14

「耐性格差」は固定化するのか―成長の持続性を見極める3つの評価軸
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
見極める軸 ポイント 代表指標 含意
波及 成長の果実は広がるか
◼ 生産性
◼ 実質賃金
◼ AI導入率
◼ 中小企業収益
波及すれば成長の裾野が拡大
しなければ成長の偏りが固定化
機会 「持たざる側」も次の成長機会に参加できるか
◼ 労働移動
◼ 新規参入
◼ 資金調達格差
◼ 人的資本投資
参加できれば格差は流動的
できなければ勝者・敗者が固定化
底上げ 格差が残る中でも、弱い主体の生活水準は改善するか
◼ ジニ係数（再分配）
◼ リスキリング
◼ 資産形成
◼ 社会移動
改善すれば社会的受容性を維持
しなければ分断圧力が強まる
問われるのは格差そのものではなく、偏在が成長の持続性を損なうか
⇒成長の果実が広がり、次の機会と生活水準の改善につながるかが焦点
評
価
軸
と
そ
の
含
意
問
題
設
定
「波及・機会・底上げ」を通じて、成長の持続性をモニタリング
偏在化する成長の果実「新常態」下の金融市場中東危機長期化、加速するAI・レジリエンス投資

### Page 15

参考①（国家間）：競争力の基盤は国ごとに偏在、資源高は日本に逆風
◼ 国家の競争力は、資源・製造能力・技術力・資金動員力・市場規模など複数の基盤に左右
― 日本は製造能力・技術力に強みを持つ一方、資源面の脆弱性から資源高局面では交易条件が悪化。また、潤沢な資金
（金融資産）を有しているものの、国内成長分野への資金動員力に課題
― 台湾・韓国では資源高で輸入価格が上昇する一方、半導体などAI関連財の価格上昇が輸出価格を押し上げ、交易条件
を下支え
国家の競争力を支える5つの基盤
競争力を支える基盤 強みを有する国・地域
①資源
エネルギー・重要鉱物などを安定的に確保できる力
米国、中東、
中国、豪州
②製造能力
戦略財を大量・安定的に生産できる力

新技術・サービスを生み出す力
米国、中国、

中国
⑤市場規模
大きな内需を背景に新技術を普及・量産できる力

参考②（企業・家計間）：生産性向上に遅れる賃金、低下する労働分配率
◼ 日本では時間当たり生産性が上昇する一方、1人当たり実質賃金の伸びは限定的
― 働き方改革など労働時間の短縮も両者の乖離に影響。交易条件悪化による所得流出も実質賃金を下押しする一因に
― 対して、AIブームの恩恵が大きい米国や韓国では、労働生産性と実質賃金がともに上昇する好循環

◼ 日本の労働分配率は主要国・地域と比較しても低下傾向。企業収益の賃金への還元が今後の焦点
― 米国や韓国においても足元で低下がみられるが、AI需要の拡大による企業収益の伸びが主因
― 日本では雇用者報酬の伸びが緩やかであり、生産性向上の成果を賃金に繋げられるかどうかが課題
（注） 労働生産性は総労働時間あたりの実質GDP、実質賃金は雇用者1人あたり
（出所） OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
労働生産性と実質賃金 主要国・地域における労働分配率の推移
（注） 自営業者の労働所得を調整した雇用者報酬の名目GDP比。韓国の2024年以降は
OECDのデータを用いて延長
（出所） 欧州委員会、OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 17

参考③（企業間）：金利上昇への耐性は企業規模・業種で大きな格差
◼ 日本企業の投下資本利益率（ROIC）は規模・業種間で大きな格差
― 総じてみれば、企業規模が小さいほどROICが低い傾向（中堅企業は例外）。特に小規模企業は1.5％と低水準
― 業種別では、製造業の輸送用機械や、非製造業の不動産、電力・ガス、運輸・郵便、医療・福祉などでROICが低い傾向。
なお、情報通信や宿泊・飲食は大企業のROICが相対的に高い一方、小規模企業が低く、業種内の格差が大きい
◼ インタレストカバレッジレシオ（ICR）も規模・業種間で格差。ROIC・ICRともに低い規模・業種ほど金利上昇に脆弱な構造
― ICRも規模が小さいほど低位（中堅企業は例外）。業種別では、不動産、電力・ガス、運輸・郵便、医療・福祉等が低水準
（注） 投下資本利益率（ROIC）＝税引き後営業利益÷（有利子負債＋自己資本）。大企業
は資本金10億円以上、中堅企業は1億円以上10億円未満、中小企業は1千万円以上
1億円未満、小規模企業は1千万円未満

（出所） 財務省「法人企業統計調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（注） インタレストカバレッジレシオ（ICR）＝営業利益÷支払利息。大企業は資本金10億円
以上、中堅企業は1億円以上10億円未満、中小企業は1千万円以上1億円未満、小

低所得世帯 高所得世帯
参考④（家計間）：金融資産を保有する層と持たざる層の間でインフレ耐性に格差
◼ 所得階級によって資産形成余力に格差。低所得世帯ほど資産形成が進みにくく、インフレ耐性が脆弱
― 高所得世帯では貯蓄余力が大きい一方、低所得世帯では物価高による生活費の増加が重石となり、貯蓄率は低水準
― 低所得世帯では金融資産の蓄積が進みにくく、インフレによる実質所得の目減りを資産所得で補う余地も限定的
◼ 資産形成余力に加え、資産選択も二極化。インフレ下では資産格差が拡大し、インフレ耐性の差も広がりやすい構造
― 余裕資金のある高所得世帯は収益性重視のリスク資産投資に積極的。一方、中低所得世帯はリスク資産投資に消極的
― インフレは資産形成余力と資産選択の違いを増幅させ、資産格差とインフレ耐性の格差がさらに拡大する可能性
（注） 勤労者世帯の平均貯蓄率。平均貯蓄率＝貯蓄純増 ÷ 可処分所得 × 100。対象

世帯・項目の違いにより、GDPベースの貯蓄率とは一致しない
（出所） 総務省「家計調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
所得別にみた貯蓄率（2021～25年平均） 年収別にみた金融資産の選択基準（2021～25年）
（注） 対象は金融資産を保有する二人以上世帯
（出所） J-FLEC「家計の金融行動に関する世論調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（１）米国：AIによる景気押し上げは当面継続。物価高から FRBは一時的に政策金利を引き上げへ
◼ 2026・27年の実質GDP成長率はいずれも前年比＋2.2％と予想
― 設備投資は、2027年入り後に巡航速度並みの伸び率へ回帰。ハイパースケーラーは投資効率の低下を意識し、AIの普
及ペースを見極めながら投資を継続
― 個人消費は減税政策や資産効果により底堅く推移。2027年入り後はインフレ減速に伴う実質所得の改善も支えに
◼ FRBは関税引き上げや原油高によるインフレ上振れリスクを警戒し、2026年内に追加で1回の利上げ。関税・原油高の影
響が一巡した後、 物価の十分な減速が確認される2027年後半以降に2回の利下げを予想

◼ 足元の景気は堅調。引き続き、AIを中心とする設備投資が景気のけん引役に
― 4～6月期のGDP成長率は前期比年率＋2.2％と巡航速度を上回る結果。設備投資に加え、 株高に伴う資産効果や

米国：低採用・低離職で人材獲得競争は限定的。足元の物価上昇には賃金以外の要因が影響

（２）欧州：2026年後半は財政拡張が下支え。2027年はインフレ減速で潜在成長率を上回る成長
◼ 2026年後半は、熱波を受けた生産抑制やインフレによる消費下押しなど逆風はありつつも、財政拡張が下支え

### Page 32

欧州：熱波による河川水位低下が景気下押し要因に
◼ 河川の水位低下による生産下振れが7～9月期の成長率を下押し
― 高温と少雨を背景に欧州各地では河川の水位が低下。内陸物流において重要な役割を担うライン川の一部地点では、6
月以降、船舶の積載量に制約が生じる水位が継続
― ライン川の水位がおおよそ100㎝を切ったあたりから10㎝低下するごとにドイツの生産が0.4％程度減少すると試算
― 実際、船舶の輸送機能低下による原材料の供給制約を受けて化学・鉄鋼等の一部素材業種では生産抑制の動き。7～
9月期の経済下押し要因に
ライン川の水位とドイツ鉱工業生産の関係
（注） 閾値モデルを用いてライン川の水位がドイツ鉱工業生産に与える影響を推計。縦軸
の鉱工業生産は景気要因をコントロール（ドイツ鉱工業生産を世界の鉱工業生産に

欧州：エネルギー・食料品価格の上昇が、さらなるインフレ期待の上昇を招くリスク
◼ 天然ガスや農作物価格上昇の影響は今年後半から来年のインフレ押し上げ要因に
― 熱波による発電所の停止や農作物の不作懸念等を背景に、欧州の天然ガス・農産物の先物価格は上昇。これらの価格
上昇がインフレに与える影響は、先行き1年程度残存する計算

― 需給が緩和するにつれ、エネルギー・食料品そのものによる物価押し上げ幅は縮小へ。ただし、インフレ期待の上昇を招

### Page 36

（３）新興国：中国は景気減速が継続。アジアは原油調達の安定や AIブームが追い風に
◼ 中国は景気の減速傾向が続く見通し。2026年の成長率は4.3％、2027年は3.8％まで低下すると予想
― 内需は力強さを欠く。消費は補助金の反動で低迷が継続。投資も過当競争や地方財政難を受けて振るわず
― 外需は景気を下支え。EVなどの製品で輸出攻勢が続き、純輸出がプラスに寄与
◼ アジアでは原油の調達不安が後退、AIブームが景気をけん引。一方、インフレ・金利上昇圧力の高まりには留意が必要
― 原油調達は、代替輸入やバイオ燃料等の活用で懸念が後退。AIブームによる財輸出や域内投資のけん引が継続
― 他方、原油高の再燃やAIブームの継続がインフレ圧力を高める構図。金融政策は引き締め方向へ緩やかにシフト

中国：政府目標は達成圏内だが成長の勢いは弱い。内需が力強さを欠くなか、デフレ圧力は継続
◼ 2026年上期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.7％。政府目標（＋4.5％～5.0％）の範囲内だが、内需の弱さが目立つ結果
― 4～6月期は同＋4.3％と2022年Q4（＋3.0％）以来の低水準。中東情勢を受けた供給ショックもあり、最終消費と総資本
形成が前期から減速。内需の弱さを純輸出（外需）が補う構図は変わらず

中国：原油輸入量は大幅減も、依然供給不足には至らず。代替燃料を用いた生産も拡大
◼ ホルムズ海峡の封鎖により、原油の輸入量は2ケタ減が続く。ただ、需要の低迷もあり供給不足には陥らず
― 海上輸送による原油輸入（3～9月）は封鎖前（10月～2月）対比▲45.0％。ロシアからの代替輸入も限定的
― ガソリンの消費量は3～8月に前年比▲8.8％、ディーゼルは同▲10.7％と低迷しており、需給のひっ迫を回避

― 加えて、国内生産量が豊富な石炭化学によるエチレン生産も強化しており、ナフサクラッカーの制約を緩和

中国：消費・投資の低迷が続く一方、輸出と生産は堅調。外需に成長を依存する構図が継続
◼ 消費・投資は減速に歯止めかからず。内需は一段と低迷
― 投資は前年比▲10％超の減少が継続。不動産不況、過当競争、地方財政難によるインフラ投資減速が背景
― 小売も減速傾向が継続。補助金の反動による自動車販売の減少等が下押し
― 一方、生産は引き続き底堅く推移。堅調な輸出を追い風に、コンピューター・通信機器などを中心に高い伸び継続
◼ 輸出はAIブームと自動車の輸出攻勢が継続。低迷する内需を外需の伸びで支える構図
― 輸出拡大は産業政策による高度化の成果と言える一方、貿易不均衡の拡大に対する懸念も（後述）

中国：好調な輸出の一方で輸入は停滞。背景に「自立自強」の進展
◼ 輸入は2010年代中盤以降に頭打ち。「自立自強」による輸入代替の進展が背景にあると考えられる
― かつて加工貿易が主流であった頃は、輸出製品を生産するため中間財や資本財の輸入が増え、先進国にも恩恵
― 世界の輸出入に占める中国のシェアは、輸出がほぼ一貫して上昇している一方、輸入は2010年代中盤以降に頭打ち
― 世界の輸入に占める中国のシェアを品目別に見ても、2024年は多くの業種で2015年からシェアが縮小
― 輸出伸長、輸入停滞の結果、貿易不均衡が拡大。先進各国はこれに懸念表明。貿易摩擦が再び強まるおそれも

アジア：原油高とAIブーム影響で生産コストが膨張。消費者価格への転嫁でインフレ圧力が高まる
◼ 企業の生産コストは増大。原油高を受けてエネルギーや石油由来製品の価格が急騰するほか、AIブーム下のメモリ高騰を
背景に、コンピューターや電気機器類の価格にも上昇圧力
― 主要国の生産者物価は総じて上昇ペースが加速。石油や有機化学製品、コンピューター類の品目が押し上げ
◼ 生産コストは消費者価格に波及。原油高がエネルギー費目を押し上げるほか、スマホやPCの価格も一部でインフレ要因に

― 幅広い地域でエネルギー部門がインフレ率を押し上げ。ガソリンや燃料（LPガス等）のほか、航空運賃の上昇が寄与
― 寄与度は小さいものの、スマホやPCなどの電子機器価格も上昇。メモリ価格の高騰が最終品価格に波及している模様
消費者物価：原油高・AIブーム影響

◼ インフレ圧力と通貨安のもと、金融政策は引き締めにシフト。ただし、内需下押し懸念から政策スタンスは域内でまちまちに

（４）日本：26年度は中東情勢影響のなかでも堅調に推移。 27年度は政策が消費・投資を押し上げ
◼ 2026年度の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋0.8％と予測。中東情勢緊迫化の影響を受ける一方、AI関連需要が下支え
― 中東情勢緊迫化は、原油価格上昇による交易利得・損失の悪化（実質的な所得の海外流出）や先行き不透明感の高ま
りを通じ、消費・投資を抑制。一方、AI・半導体関連輸出の拡大や投資促進税制の開始を受け、堅調な成長に
◼ 2027年度は同＋1.3％と予測。消費税減税の開始による個人消費の押し上げに加え、国内投資拡大がけん引

― 消費税減税が物価上昇の抑制を通じて実質賃金の改善要因に。また、政府の「強く豊かな日本投資枠」による戦略17分
野への支援開始に伴い、設備投資の拡大が成長率を押し上げると予想
（注） 網掛けは予測値

### Page 49

日本：コアCPIは2026年度末にかけて一時前年比＋3％程度に。日銀は2％まで利上げ継続
◼ AIブームの恩恵を受け、企業業績は中東情勢影響のなかでも改善傾向を維持
― 企業の好業績や人手不足の強さを背景に、2027年度の春闘でも5％程度の高い賃上げ率が続くと予想
◼ コアCPI（生鮮食品を除く総合）は、2026年度末にかけて一時的に前年比＋3％程度へ加速
― 原油価格の上昇が消費者物価を押し上げ。物価上振れリスクに対応するため、日銀は2％まで追加利上げを実施へ
― 2027年度に入ると、原油価格が徐々に落ち着くほか、利上げがインフレ率の上昇を抑制。加えて消費税減税が物価を一
段と下押しし、コアCPIが一時的に前年比＋1％台半ばへ低下する見込み
（注） 1. 網掛けは予測値。変化率は、水準のデータからみずほ総合研究所調査部が計算した値であり、公表値と一致しないことがある
2. 経常利益は法人企業統計調査の全規模・全産業（除く金融・保険）ベース
3. 消費者物価の「除く制度要因」は、政府による電気・ガス料金支援、ガソリン補助・暫定税率廃止、高校授業料無償化、消費税減税等の影響を除いて計算

日本：2026年度の成長率を上方修正。AI関連を中心とする輸出の堅調さが主な要因
◼ 2026年度の実質GDP成長率（＋0.8％）は、前回6月見通し（＋0.5％）対比で0.3％Ptの上方修正
― 米国を中心とする旺盛なAI関連需要の恩恵を受け、財輸出が堅調に拡大するとの見方が、26年度上方修正の主因
― 輸入価格上昇を受けた交易利得・損失の悪化（実質的な所得の海外流出）は、個人消費や設備投資などの抑制要因と
して残るものの、輸出の堅調な拡大が景気下押し圧力を一部緩和
◼ 2027年度は、消費税減税の開始や投資促進政策の効果発現により、引き続き高い成長を予想
― ただし、インフレ率の上振れを受けて実質所得の伸びがやや弱まることなどから、前回6月対比で小幅に下方修正

日本：政府が掲げる「2040年度：250兆円」の国内投資目標は、概ね実現可能な範囲
◼ 投資促進税制（26～28年度）に加え、戦略17分野を中心として「強く豊かな日本投資枠」（27～40年度）による支援を実施
― 「強く豊かな日本投資枠」は、27年度概算要求ベース（一般・特別会計）で14.2兆円と大規模
◼ 設備投資は自然体でも2040年度に230兆円へ到達する計算。政府目標は概ね現実的な範囲
― 直近実績を踏まえ「自然体」の伸びを＋4％とすると、目標達成に必要な押し上げ分は累計125兆円（年平均8兆円強）。

日本：輸入増の影響を考慮しても、投資拡大が名目GDPの水準を＋0.5％程度押し上げ
◼ 日本の資本財供給では輸入浸透度が上昇。グローバルな資本財需給ひっ迫は国内供給力強化の足かせに
― とりわけ、電気機械・情報通信機械では輸入依存度の高まりが顕著
― 半導体等を中心にグローバルな需給ひっ迫が続くとみられ、AI・半導体分野等を中心に投資完了には時間
◼ もっとも、輸入増による需要の海外漏出を考慮しても、国内生産増を通じたGDP押し上げ効果は相応の規模
― 投資需要が期待通り実現すれば、投資促進策がない場合と比べて名目GDP水準を平均＋0.5％程度押し上げ

日本：原油の代替調達は継続。ただし、油価再上昇や輸送コスト増加が物価上振れ要因に
◼ 今後も原油の代替調達が継続する見通し。時期により調達先が変化するものの、原油の供給不足は回避

日本：コスト高を受け、コアCPIは26年度末にかけて加速。27年度は消費税減税が下押し要因に

― 原油高による前年比押し上げの縮小や、日銀の利上げ効果、円安一服による輸入コスト上昇圧力の緩和が影響

日本：26年度後半の個人消費は緩慢な回復に留まるも、 27年度は消費税減税を追い風に拡大
◼ 2026年度後半はインフレ加速により、実質賃金の伸びが鈍化。個人消費は緩慢な回復に留まる見通し
― 個人消費は、良好な所得環境にもかかわらず低調。背景には、中東情勢の悪化に伴う消費者マインドの弱含み
― マインドは足元で回復の兆しも、26年度後半はインフレ加速が消費を抑制。消費税減税前の買い控えも下押し
◼ 2027年度の個人消費は消費税減税を追い風に拡大。買い控え後の反動増もあり、1％を上回る高い伸びとなる見通し

― 29年度の減税終了時には消費税率の再引き上げが物価を押し上げるも、新たな給付策で実質所得の落ち込みを緩和

金融政策：インフレ圧力の下で価格転嫁行動が積極化。日銀は 27年央に2％まで利上げ
◼ コスト高・供給不安定化のもと、日本企業の価格転嫁行動に積極化の兆候
― 民間調査では幅広い業種における価格転嫁短期化が示されているほか、足元では価格転嫁の動きに広がり。値上げ行
動の積極化は、収益改善を支えるとともに、物価上昇圧力の定着に繋がる可能性
◼ 物価上振れ警戒モードの金融政策運営が継続。9月会合後も日銀は短いインターバルで利上げ
― 9月会合後の会見で植田総裁は、物価引き上げから物価上振れ抑制へと「政策の局面が変化した」と説明

― 27年央にかけ四半期に1回程度のペースで利上げ。堅調な資金需要・投資計画を踏まえ、ターミナルレートは2％と予想
（注） 品目別に前年比の伸びを計算し、3カ月前から加速した品目の割合から減速した品
目の割合を差し引いて算出した指数

長期金利：市場の政策金利織り込みは2％に収束へ。長期金利は3％超の水準で高止まり
◼ 2026年度の長期金利は、物価上振れ懸念と利上げ織り込みの進展を背景に上昇
― タームプレミアム（上乗せ金利）は物価上振れ懸念から夏場にかけて大きく拡大したが、その後は緩やかな拡大ペースに
◼ 2027年度以降の長期金利は横ばい圏で推移。3％超の水準が常態化する見通し
― 物価上振れ懸念が後退するもとで、行き過ぎた利上げ織り込みは徐々に縮小
― 一方、日銀のバランスシート縮小を背景にイールドカーブの正常化が進展。日銀の大規模な国債保有で抑制されていた
タームプレミアムが緩やかに再拡大し、利上げ織り込みのはく落による金利低下圧力をおおむね相殺

（注） 政策金利織り込みは、今後10年間の政策金利に対する市場織り込みの平均。網掛け
部は期末政策金利との差分。2026年Q2までNakajima (2025)による推計値、2026年
Q3期以降みずほ総研推計値
（出所） Nakajima, J. (2025). “Impact of US monetary policy spillovers and yield curve
control policy,”、一上響・上野陽一 (2013). 「ゼロ金利下におけるタームプレミアム
の推計：日米英の長期金利の分析」、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

為替：ドル円レートのレンジは2022年頃に円安方向へシフト。今後も構造的な円安圧力が残存
◼ 長い目でみれば、ドル円レートは2022年ごろを境にレンジが円安方向にシフト
― 2000～21年のドル円のレンジは1ドル＝80円～120円程度だったが、2022年以降は1ドル＝130円～170円程度に
― 背景には、①高い米金利、②円の需給環境の悪化、③日本のインフレノルムの定着など
◼ 先行きも、1ドル＝150円程度を中心とした円安水準が継続しやすい経済環境に
― 27年度は、短期的には「米利下げ」＋「日銀利上げ」で日米金利差が縮小し、円高要因に
― ただし、原油高が続くもとでの日本の貿易赤字、期待インフレの高止まり等が円安要因となり、大幅円高は見込まず
（出所） LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

• 日米金利差が縮小し、短期的には

• もっとも長期的には、日米成長率格
差から米金利が相対的に高水準
短期：円高
長期：円安
需給
• 短期的にはAI関連輸出が下支えす

• 原油高長期化で、輸入は高止まり
• デジタル赤字は拡大へ
• 対外直接投資も増加基調継続へ

期待

• 2010年代までは0%台だったが、

• 期待インフレ率の高止まりは、購買
力平価の観点では円安要因

市場焦點
美國
• 9月ISM服務業PMI降至54.9，雖商業活動與新訂單放緩 ，但就業回升至 50.1、訂單積壓增加 ，
顯示服務需求仍具韌性；另一方面，價格指數升至74.0，創2022年7月以來新高，供應商交貨
速度亦放緩 ，反映燃料 、原物料及供應鏈成本升高 ，景氣未明顯轉弱之際 ，通膨壓力反而重
新升溫；同時全球長債殖利率同步走高 ，AI基建投資延續企業成長動能 ，也提高資本需求 。
整體而言 ，美國經濟尚未明顯轉弱 ，但通膨與長端利率壓力同步升高 ，使金融條件維持偏緊 。
中國
• 彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與 AI普及下，中國產業快速發展出現供給過剩 ，產業獲利
能力面臨挑戰，未來三年中國AI產業將可能處於虧損狀態，不過即使模型價格下降，AI使用量
增加將帶動Token需求大幅成長，其中具有企業客戶、專有資料及生態優勢的AI龍頭，將比單

純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。
黃金
• 歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵 ，加上長期通膨風險升溫 ，宏觀不確定性讓實體黃金
市場逆勢吸金 。本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易 ，實體黃金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中
期的支撐。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以投資組
合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。

美國9月ISM服務需求略降、成本壓力升高，景氣韌性與通膨風險並存
• 服務業維持擴張，需求略降但就業回穩：9月ISM服務業指數降至54.9(前月55.4)，連續27個月位於擴張區間；商業活動
指數降至56.5、新訂單降至 59.8，顯示服務需求較前月放緩 ，但就業指數回升至 50.1、重返擴張區間 ，訂單積壓升至
56.6，顯示內需仍具韌性，服務業尚未出現明顯轉弱訊號。
• 成本與供應鏈壓力升高 ，通膨降溫面臨干擾： 價格指數升至 74.0，創2022年7月以來新高 ，且為7個月來第6次高於70；
供應商交貨指數升至53.2，顯示交貨速度放緩，燃料、柴油、鋼材、銅及記憶體等成本上升，加上供應受限，使服務業
投入成本壓力擴大，通膨降溫進程面臨干擾。
• 市場觀察：美國服務業為經濟成長的重要支撐，目前經濟呈現「需求仍強、通膨升溫」的拉鋸格局，雖服務業景氣支撐

美國企業獲利，但成本上升可能推升利率波動，投資上宜偏向美國短中天期投資級債，兼顧較高收益率與利率風險控管；
股票則可聚焦具定價能力、獲利與現金流穩健的優質企業。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6 資料來源：ISM Services PMI Report， 2026.10.6
ISM服務業指數分項指標變動對比表ISM服務業PMI主要分項指數變化

全球利率同步走高、AI投資持續擴張，長端美債壓力仍高
• 全球長債殖利率同步走高，反映利率環境重新定價：美國財政部長貝森特指出，近期美債殖利率攀升與德國、日本等主
要債市同步上行，反映全球資本成本重新定價，而非市場對美國資產失去信心；加上目前尚未出現資金大規模撤離美債
並轉向其他主權債券的現象，因此債市波動較偏向全球利率環境調整，而非金融系統性風險。
• AI投資增加資金需求，支撐較高利率環境：貝森特亦駁斥AI泡沫論，認為微軟、Alphabet及Meta等大型科技企業仍以
實際獲利與現金流支撐持續擴大AI資本支出，不同於過去缺乏基本面的科技泡沫；龐大的資料中心 、電力與半導體投資
需求提高整體資金成本，也成為推升長天期利率的重要因素之一。
• 市場觀察：目前市場經濟近況屬於利率高檔、成長尚穩的情境中，因而投資策略建議配置大型科技股、AI基建供應鏈及

受惠利差環境的金融股具相對優勢；債券配置則以中短天期投資級債優先，避免長天期債券受利率上行壓力侵蝕。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6
長端殖利率維持今年以來高檔
資料來源：兆豐銀行整理， 2026.10.6

• 美國8月貿易逆差擴大至2025年初以來最高水平：美國提高進口關稅後，貿易失衡問題仍未明顯改善，商務部數據顯示，8
月貿易逆差擴大13.7%至1,056億美元，且超出市場預期的1,021億美元。經通膨調整後，8月商品貿易逆差擴大至1,147億美
元，同樣為2025年3月以來最大。
• 觀察美國本土投資能否進一步轉化為出口能力：AI建設帶動半導體與設備進口，加上能源成本上升及關稅政策干擾，推升進
口額至歷史新高。不過，強勁內需也顯示消費與企業投資仍具支撐力。隨著第三季財報季即將展開，根據Factset數據，分析
師預期第三季標普500企業獲利年增約29.5%，加上企業獲利預期持續上修，強化投資人對高利率環境下企業基本面的信心。
投資策略上，相較於單筆投資，此時的高成長與高波動的環境，更適合分批布局的定期定額策略來參與市場。

美國進口創下解放日以來的最高水平

• 實質薪資持續成長，內需復甦動能增強：日本8月實質現金收入年增1.5%，連續第8個月成長，名目工資與基本工資均年增
3.8%，顯示薪資成長已逐步抵銷通膨對購買力的侵蝕；剔除獎金、加班費及抽樣偏差後，全職勞工薪資仍成長2.8%，反映
薪資上漲具有一定持續性。實質所得改善有助家庭消費與內需回升，進一步支撐企業營收與獲利展望。
• 薪資與通膨並行，日股中長期仍具配置價值：薪資持續上升有助日本形成薪資增加、進而消費擴大，導向企業營收改善的正
向循環，同時也強化日本央行持續推進貨幣政策正常化的條件。雖然升息可能推升日圓與資金成本，但金融股可受惠於利差
擴大，且企業治理改革、AI投資及自動化需求仍提供獲利支撐。近期日股AI與半導體族群已明顯走強，顯示市場仍重視企業
獲利成長；中長期建議維持日股正向看法，聚焦金融、AI、機器人、自動化及電力設備等具結構性成長題材的企業。

市
場
回
顧
中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境
• 中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境 ：彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與
AI普及下，中國AI產業快速發展 ，但也逐漸出現供給過剩 ，使產業獲
利能力面臨挑戰 。開源模式雖有助於降低技術門檻 、應用普及 ，但也
使技術優勢難以長期維持 ，加上中國市場長期習慣低價甚至免費的數
位服務，多數AI業者難以透過訂閱模式獲利，轉而依賴利潤較低的API
服務作為主要收入來源 。目前中國已備案大型語言模型 (LLM)數量達
988個，包括DeepSeek、Kimi及智譜AI等新創企業快速崛起，進一步
加劇市場競爭。此外API服務逐漸被視為基礎公用服務，即使AI Agent
帶動詞元(Token)需求快速成長，也未必能同步提升獲利，由於開源模
型可由不同雲端業者部署 ，市場進入門檻降低 ，業者更難建立長期競

爭優勢。
• 後市展望： 考量龐大資本支出與持續價格競爭 ，未來三年中國 AI產業
整體仍可能處於虧損狀態 ，市場將形成「美國閉源、高毛利」與「中
國開源 、低成本 」模式 ，即使模型價格下降 ，AI 使用量增加將帶動
Token需求大幅成長 ，進而推升資料中心 、雲端服務及 AI晶片需求 ，
從投資角度來看，基礎設施供應商(晶片、伺服器、資料中心、雲端)受
惠程度可能高於模型開發商；而具有企業客戶 、專有資料及生態優勢
的AI龍頭，將比單純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。

人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台，有望提振中國經濟
和市場信心
• 人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台 ，有望提振中國經濟和市場信心 ：日前中國推出購房貸款貼息政策及下調
PSL(抵押補充貸款)利率，代表穩增長政策進一步加碼 ，有助於提振市場信心與經濟預期 ，此次政策不僅支持房地產市場 ，也
將「六張網」建設、科技創新、民營及小微企業 ，納入重點扶持範圍 ，透過增加再貸款額度 ，與提高支持比例 ，帶動投資、
就業及消費回升。短期A股有望在政策推動下出現築底反彈，其中基建投資、製造業投資、房市銷售及居民消費有機會改善，
同時PSL(抵押補充貸款)降息有助於流動性環境邊際寬鬆 ，降低市場對經濟增長的疑慮 ，後市來看企業獲利與信用環境有望逐
步修復，進一步鞏固A股慢牛格局。投資建議上房貸貼息政策有利房地產、建材、家居等產業鏈，金融業受惠資金成本下降與

信貸回升，食品飲料、零售、家電及服務消費則可望受惠於居民消費意願提升。

中國、日本減持美國公債的背後邏輯與差異
• 橋水基金創始人達利歐警告 ，美國公債正面臨中日兩國減持的風險 ，數據顯示這項警告已成事實 。2026年前七個月，中國
美國公債持倉減少660億美元至6,180億美元；同期日本持倉亦減少820億美元至1.1兆美元，扣除價格變動的估值效應後，
兩國實質持倉均大幅下滑。然而，兩國削減美國公債的背後動機並不相同，這將導致未來的減持軌跡出現明顯分化。
• 中國戰略性長期去美元化：1.降低地緣政治風險與轉向資產多元化 ，隨著人民幣在國際貿易結算中使用增加 ，市場流動性提
升，中國對美元儲備依賴度顯著下降。2.美債財政可持續性隱憂，美國政府債務持續攀升，引發對美國長期財政健康疑慮。
3.根據彭博經濟研究模型預測，在漸進式減持情境下，中國的美元儲備(以美債與機構債為主)到2050年可能減少約50%。

• 中國搭配戰術性節奏調整 (短期緩和因素)：儘管長期趨勢向下 ，但目前中美利差顯著 (美中30年期公債利差超過 350個基點)，
在美債殖利率維持高位期間，過快減持意味著實現債券價格下跌的虧損，且可能加劇人民幣升值壓力。因此，中國短中期內
傾向採取「戰略性減持、戰術性放緩」的靈活步調。
美國公債持倉：中國 vs 日本
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.8，美國財政部
中國預計將減少美元儲備

日本受政策利率與本土資本回流驅動
• 與中國的結構性考量不同，日本的減持主要出自週期性因素與匯率干預需求。
• 日本央行貨幣政策轉鷹： 日本央行正加快升息腳步 。隨著日債殖利率上揚 (30年期日債殖利率升至約 4.2%)，美日利差持續
收窄，吸引日本國內機構資金回流本土主權債市場。
• 匯率干預政策：日本為防範日圓過度貶值，日本財務省傾向出售部分海外美債資產以籌集外匯干預資金。若日圓再次面臨壓
力，此類拋售干預可能持續。因此，隨著日本央行逐步走向政策正常化，日本對美債的持倉大概會延續下滑軌跡。
• 小結與投資建議：美國公債作為全球無風險資產的地位正面臨結構性挑戰，中國的「長期去美元化」與日本的「本土利率回
升資金回流」將形成雙重壓力，這意味著美債殖利率短期內難大幅下行，美國財政部發債成本居高不下，進而推高全球資本

的融資成本。投資債券配置建議採取「中短天期＋階梯式配置」策略，長天期美國公債(20年以上)受發債供給過剩及中日兩
國長期減持影響，價格波動較大。建議投資人適度降低長天期美國公債暴險，轉向防禦性較佳的中短天期美國公債或高品質
高評級公司債，以鎖定當前較高殖利率同時控管利率風險。
殖利率差：美元與人民幣 vs 美元與日圓
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

黃金市場資金流向分歧，實體投資有望支撐中長期金價表現
• 利率與匯率因素，壓抑金價近期表現：9月以來，美國公債殖利率飆升、美元指數走揚，由於黃金本身不孳息，資金傾向轉
往固定收益市場，疊加美元走強壓抑國際購買意願，金價自8月底高點至今已下修約11%。
• 9月資金流向分歧，實體投資強、期貨市場弱：根據世界黃金協會統計，9月在金價下跌之際，全球實體黃金ETF卻吸引大量
資金流入，流入約100億美元(67噸)。另一方面，COMEX黃金期貨市場投機部位大幅下降 ，避險基金與其他機構投資人的
多頭部位明顯減少，造成期貨市場賣壓增加，進一步推動金價下跌。
• 實體黃金市場逆勢吸金反應投資人的避險需求：歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵，加上美伊衝突未解及AI算力需求強
勁，長期通膨風險升溫，宏觀的不確定性讓實體黃金市場逆勢吸金。從數據上來看，本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易，實體黃

金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中期的支撐 。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以
投資組合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，2026.10.7。

付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

## Document 045: 2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

US Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2026 年 10 月 FOMC プレビュー 
～インフレ認識と AI 投資の持続性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 FOMC（10/27～28 開催）では政策金利が 3.75～4.00％に据え置かれる可能性が高い。
FRB は 9 月に約 3 年振りとなる利上げに踏み切った一方、追加の政策調整を急がず、今後の
経済指標を見極める姿勢を示すとみられる。 
○ ウォーシュ議長の記者会見では、予想を上振れるインフレ動向に関する評価、及び一部のAI
クラウド業者による信用不安が今後の設備投資に与える影響を巡って、何らかの見解が示さ
れるかが注目される。もっとも、市場で織り込まれる12 月 FOMCの利上げを巡っては、具体
的な言及を避ける可能性が高い。 
○ FRBは5つのタスクフォースのうちの生産性と雇用（Productivity and Jobs）部会におい
て、11月16 日にサンフランシスコで公聴会を開催する予定だ。年末までに取りまとめられ
る提言や議論の進捗に関して、ウォーシュ議長から何らかの言及があるかが注目される。

### Page 2

2/5 
・据え置きが見込まれる 10 月FOMC 
2026 年10月FOMC（10/27～28開催）において、FRBは賛成多数で政策金利（3.75～4.00%）を据
え置く可能性が高い。FRBは 9 月FOMCにて米国経済の堅調な推移やインフレ高止まりを踏まえ、23
年 7 月以来となる0.25%の利上げに踏み切った。また、同時に公表されたドットチャートで年内に
追加で 1回の利上げ見通しが示された。8 月CPI及びFRBのタカ派的なスタンスを踏まえて、金融市
場は連続利上げの可能性を徐々に織り込んでいったものの、FRB高官は9月末から10 月初めにか
けてこれに慎重なスタンスを示した。例えば、9/29にウィリアムズ総裁は「急ぐ必要はなく、情
報を集める時間がある」 、10/1 にジェファーソン副議長は「 （政策判断には）もう少し時間がかか
るかもしれない」とそれぞれ述べた。 
この結果、本稿執筆時点におけるFF金利先物市場（Fed Watch）において、83.9%の確率で金利
据え置きが見込まれる一方、0.25%ptの利上げ予想は16.1%に留まる（資料1）。なお、金融市場は
12 月FOMCにおける0.25%の利上げに加えて、2027年末に向けて更に 2回程度の利上げを織り込ん
でいる（資料 2）。この場合の合計利上げ幅は+1.0%ptとなり、9月時点のドットチャート（26年に
計 0.5%ptの利上げを行い、27 年は据え置き）を上回る。もっとも、過去の利上げ局面ではそれが
1～2 回に留まることは稀であり、金融市場はこうした過去の事例も一部に織り込んでいるとみら
れる（詳細は 2026年 9月 18日付け「ＦＲＢの過去の利上げサイクルと長期金利の行方」）。

資料 1：10月 FOMCにおける利上げ織り込み

3/5 
slight growth） 」とまとめられた。また、 「労働市場は総じて変化がなかった」と指摘した。一
方、「物価上昇は3 地区で力強く上昇し、8 地区で緩やかに上昇した。8地区のうち3地区では投
入価格の上昇圧力が強まっている」とインフレ高止まりへの懸念が強調された。また、複数の地
区でインフレによる生活費負担の増大が賃金の上昇圧力になっている可能性が指摘された。 
10 月FOMCまでには10/27に 9 月耐久財受注や10月コンファレンスボード消費者信頼感指数など
が公表されるものの、10月FOMCの政策判断に大きな影響を与える可能性は低い。

・インフレ認識、AI投資の持続性、タスクフォース 
9 月FOMCの声明文では「経済活動は堅調に拡大している」との景気認識が維持された。一方、 「イ
ンフレ率は高止まりしている（7 月時点：インフレ率は2%目標に対して高止まりしている。これは
エネルギーを含む一部セクターにおける供給ショックを通じた価格上昇を反映している） 」と、供
給ショックに関する言及が前回から削除された。10 月FOMCにおいても、こうした景気・物価認識は
概ね維持される可能性が高い。一方、物価認識に関して、足下のインフレ減速の鈍化や加速の兆し
を指摘する、或いはインフレ高止まりの背景として需要の強さに言及する場合、タカ派的なスタン
スと捉えられるだろう。 
ウォーシュ議長は記者会見において、次回 12 月FOMCに関する具体的な言及を避けるとみられる
一方、下記3 点に関して新たな見解が示されるかが焦点となろう。 
まず、足下のインフレ認識に変化があるかだ。9 月FOMCの記者会見で議長は「インフレ率は高止
まりしており、その状態はあまりに長く続いている」や「あまりに多くの品目が 3％を超えるイン
フレ率にある」と述べた。また、同会合の議事要旨では何名か（some）の参加者が「AI普及を通じ
て、需要が中期的に供給を上回り、インフレ上昇への圧力となる可能性がある」と指摘した。その
後公表された 9 月のコアCPIは予想を上振れるなど、高関税による財価格の押し上げやエネルギー
高による輸送サービスへの波及の兆しがみられる。ウォーシュ議長がこうした需要と供給双方のイ
ンフレ要因に関して、新たな見解を示すのかが注目される。 
次に、AI投資の持続性への見方だ。FOMC後の10/29に公表される7～9月期実質GDP成長率は前期
から加速すると見込まれ、AI関連を中心とした設備投資の拡大、及び資産効果（株高）による消費
の堅調さが示されるとみられる。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16 の講演において「世界経済は 5～6 か月
前に予測できなかったほどの回復力を示している」と述べ、エネルギー高や利上げに対する経済的
な耐性を強調した。一方、足下ではAIモデル開発業者などに計算資源を提供する「ネオクラウド企
業」の一部で信用不安が浮上しており、データセンターを中心としたAI関連投資が減速するとの懸
念が強まっている。ウォーシュ議長がAI投資の先行き、及び一部企業の信用不安が金融市場全体に
与える影響に関して、どのような見解を示すのかが焦点となる。 
最後に、タスクフォースの進捗状況が部分的にでも示され、2027年以降の政策運営に関するヒン
トがみられるかだ。ウォーシュ議長は就任後の6月に 5 つのタスクフォース（①コミュニケーショ
ン、②バランスシート政策、③既存データの活用、④変化する時代の生産性と雇用、⑤FRBのインフ
レ枠組み）の設置を発表し、FRB改革を推進する姿勢を示した。5つのタスクフォースは秋までに何

### Page 4

4/5 
らかの成果を得たうえで、2026 年末までにFRBへ提言をまとめる予定だ。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16
の講演にて、11 月 16 日にサンフランシスコで生産性と雇用に関する公聴会を開催すると述べてお
り、これを含むタスクフォースの進捗状況が開示されるか注目される。

クリーブランド連銀
ハマック総裁*
インフレ持続の背景として、企業投資の急拡大や資産効果を通じた
消費押し上げによる需要増を指摘した。こうした需要をある程度減
速させる必要性を強調したほか、投資需要を抑制することで雇用を
悪化させずにインフレを抑制できる可能性を指摘した
10/13
ニューヨーク連銀
ウィリアムズ総裁*
年内に追加で1回の利上げを支持した一方、2027年に関しては追加利
上げの必要はないと明言した。この背景として、来年はエネルギー
価格がインフレを押し上げないこと、及び関税の影響が一服するこ
とのほか、足下の米国経済が全体として過熱していないことを指摘
した
10/14
サンフランシスコ連銀
デイリー総裁

「（利上げが）必要となる可能性が高いが、必要な時間はある」と
述べるなど、10月FOMCの利上げに慎重な姿勢を示した。また、予想
を上振れた9月CPIを巡って、 「インフレリスクは依然として高いが、
リスクは現実に起きたわけではない」と、基調的なインフレ動向に
大きな変化が起きていないとの認識を示唆した
10/16 ウォーシュ議長*
AI関連を中心に「投資の急増」が生じていると指摘し、世界経済が
エネルギーショックに対する想定以上の強靭性を示しているとの楽
観的な見方に言及した。一方、先行きの政策決定には「予断を持た
ない」と述べるなど、10月FOMCに関するヒントは与えなかった

南亞科(2408.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
價格與⻑約重議價推升獲利，AI 需求⽀撐中⻑期成⻑  
 投資評價與建議 
受惠與記憶體供不應求持續，目前⻑約覆蓋率約50%～70%之間，合約期限從
季度到⻑達3年不等，其中，3年期合約佔總產出20%以上，有助營運能見度提
升；另隨著AI應用占比提升，有助降低記憶體景氣循環影響。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26反應⻑期合約重新簽訂之調整，ASP季增超過60%： 3Q26營收季增
61.9%至1,336.5億元，年增611.7%，受惠平均售價季增60+%，其⻑約貢獻超
過⼀半以上；位元出貨量季減中個位數百分比，⽑利率79.5%攀升至85.7%，
營業淨利率80%，季增6.3ppts，營業費用75.88億元，費用增加主因當期及未
來的員工薪酬增加，稅後淨利876.27億元，稅後EPS 25.4元，優於預期。 
4Q26供不應求延續，預期ASP持續上揚、出貨持平：展望4Q26，公司預期市
場供不應求持續，ASP持續向上，惟在位元出貨量，由於目前庫存處於低檔，
預期未來數個季度的位元出貨量將保持平穩，待4Q27新廠開始投產，預估
4Q26營收季增21.7%至1,626.7億元，⽑利率86.6%，稅後31.29元。 
随新廠產能開出，目標AI基礎設施逐步提升：新廠規劃1Q27裝機，因無大
幅增加產出的空間，加上庫存處於低水位，預期2027年初出貨量持平。南
亞科目前AI基礎設施相關營收占比已超過20%，含蓋通訊、儲存、系統管
理、eSSD以及RDIMM模組等，2030年以前目標提升至40～50%。 
 投資風險 
AI基礎建設投資放緩、客製化AI/WoW產品進度不如預期

| 營運概況  
3Q26 反應⻑期合約重新簽訂之調整 ， ASP 季增超過 60% ：3Q26 營收季增 61.9%至 1,336.5 億元 ， 年增 611.7% ，
受惠平均售價季增 60+%，其⻑約貢獻超過⼀半以上；位元出貨量季減中個位數百分比，⽑利率 79%攀升至 85.7%，
營業淨利 1,068.9 億元，營業淨利率 80%，季增 6.3ppts，營業費用 75.88 億元，費用增加主因當期及未來的員
工薪酬增加 ， 稅後淨利 876.27 億元 ， 淨利率 65.6% ， 稅後 EPS 25.4 元 ， 優於預期 。 產品結構部份 ， DDR4/LPDDR4
佔比逾 60%，DDR5 約佔 10%；此外，AI 基礎設施(含伺服器、網路、儲存及系統管理)占營收超過 20%。 
4Q26 供不應求延續，預期 ASP 持續上揚、出貨持平：展望 4Q26，公司預期市場供不應求持續，ASP 持續向上，
惟在位元出貨量，由於目前庫存處於低檔，預期未來數個季度的位元出貨量將保持平穩，直到 4Q27 新廠開始投
產，預估 4Q26 營收季增 21.7%至 1,626.7 億元，⽑利率 86.6%，稅後 31.29 元。此外，就供需市況部份，隨著 AI
運算主導 DRAM 需求成⻑ ， 延伸至網路 、 儲存及系統管理等應用 ， 推升高容量 、 高頻寬及客製化記憶體架構需求 ，
而傳統應用因受限於供給不足加上報價調漲，轉而降低規格、延⻑⽣命週期來因應；另供給則因新廠建置、設備
交期、製程導入，均需要較⻑的前置時間，預期供不應求狀況在 2027 年至 2028 年前難以解決。 
随新廠產能開出，目標 AI 基礎設施應用占比逐步提升：產能規劃方面，新廠規劃 1Q27 裝機，因無大幅增加產出
的空間，再加上庫存消耗後，正處低水位，預期 2027 年初出貨量持平，隨著 4Q27 新廠開始投產後有望逐步提
升，目標 2028 年達每月投片量３萬片，2030 年年底達到每月投片量５萬片。此外，第三代 10 奈米級製程(1C)
預計 1Q27 開始客戶驗證，並同步開發第四代(1D)、第五代(1E)製程及 EUV，均按計畫推進。另規劃於屏東科學
園區建置 3D 晶圓封裝與測試廠，預計 2028 年年底前導入矽穿孔(TSV)、晶圓堆疊等 3D IC 先進技術，未來擴建
皆以客戶⻑期合約的 AI 專案為目標。南亞科目前 AI 基礎設施相關營收占比已超過 20%，含蓋通訊、儲存、系統
管理、eSSD 以及 RDIMM 模組等，2030 年以前目標提升至 40～50%；此外，目前⻑約覆蓋率約 50%～70%之
間，合約期限從季度到⻑達 3 年不等，其中，3 年期合約佔總產出 20%以上，有助營運能見度提升；另與客戶合
作 AI 相關晶圓對晶圓技術 ASIC 專案，應用範圍涵蓋雲端 AI 運算、邊緣運算及物理 AI，已有營收貢獻，雖然目
前規模不大，後續業務發展值得關注。預估 2027 年營收 7,378.76 億元，⽑利率 87.1%，稅後 EPS 141.9 元，維
持「買進」之投資建議，目標價 710 元(3.8 倍 PBR*2027BVPS(F))。

### Page 1

Nanya Technology

Upbeat 3Q margin/ASP, and 2027 guidance 
also optimistic: lift PO to NT$800

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 800.00 TWD | Price: 527.00 TWD

3Q results confirm super-cycle even with legacy DRAM 
Nanya Tech, a pure DRAM supplier, reported very strong 3Q results (sales: NT$134bn, 
+62% QoQ / +612% YoY; OPM: 80% - similar to big-3 DRAM makers’; net margin: 66%). 
The key contributor should be upbeat ASP (up 60%+ QoQ vs consensus of 30-40%). In 
fact, Nanya Tech had already reported more than doubled 1H ASP (60%+ QoQ for two 
consecutive quarters). Thus, Street expected limited QoQ price increase in 3Q (vs 1H).  
According to Nanya Tech President Pei Ing Lee, demand for legacy DRAM products 
(DDR3 & DDR4) remained strong across IT, industrial, and enterprise applications. Mgmt 
also said legacy DRAM accounted for 80%+ of 3Q sales (vs 10% range for DDR5). Net-
net, the 3Q results confirmed super-cycle even selling mainly legacy/conventional DRAM. 
Guidance broadly bullish; no sign of a price cut 
Lee provided bullish guidance (earnings call). What we learned: (1) rising 4Q ASP, (2) 
ongoing shortage of legacy DRAM through 2027 (no immediate demand shift from 
DDR4 to DDR5), (3) new fab mass production (2027-28), (4) significant FCF generation 
(with high margin profile) despite large capex spend (NT$70bn in 2026), (5) record-high 
net cash (NT$270bn in 3Q), (6) new node development (in-house) and migration (1c, 1d, 
1e) on track, and (7) new construction plan for 3D wafer packaging/testing facilities in 
Ping Tung Science Park, Taiwan. Overall, new guidance presents further growth (capacity 
expansion) and high margin profile (favorable ASP trend). 
Lift PO to NT$800 on revisions to 2027/28 EPS estimates 
We lift our PO from NT$750 to NT$800 using the same target P/E vs earlier (9x 2027-
28E EPS). Thus, our PO revision is mostly based on 7-8% revisions to 2027-28 EPS 
estimates. We assume 5%/1ppt higher sales/OPM vs our earlier forecasts given more 
favorable conventional DRAM ASP trend. To be conservative, we assume negative EPS 
growth for 2028 but it can turn positive if the ASP remains stable. We reiterate Buy.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Nanya Technology is an affiliate of Formosa Plastics Group. 
The firm manufactures and distributes DRAM chips. 
Management focuses on non-PC applications such as 
consumer, mobile, server, autos, etc. Key customers are 
based in China and Taiwan. Samsung, SK Hynix and Micron 
are major competitors.

Investment Rationale 
Our Buy rating is based on the DRAM industry's supercycle, 
which should warrant a high-margin profile for Nanya Tech 
even in the long term. Its 3Q26 results proved record-high 
revenue (NT$134bn, more than 5x higher than the previous 
peak cycle) and 80% operating margin. Management focus 
on long-term contracts, including DDR4 for eSSD or servers, 
should also act as a catalyst. Significantly rising net cash 
even after capex hike could also support higher dividend 
payouts.

BofA Memory Indicator 
Exhibit 25: The BofA Memory Indicator still presents super-cycle – much higher than 2017-18 
peak level; of course, it slightly declined to 179 in Aug-26 (due to lower YoY growth) after 
hitting peak of 190 in Mar/Apr-26, driven by exceptionally strong DRAM/NAND spot pricing, 
rising ASPs and billings, along with Korea’s ~200%+ export growth. 
BofA Memory Indicator – back to upturn level since Oct-2025 and hit highest peak in Mar/Apr-26 
(back-tested)

Note: Our indicator had previously been capped at ~140, but the recent unprecedented surge—driven by memory spot prices, ASPs, and 
billings—has led us to raise the ceiling (up to 240 levels) to reflect a more accurate relative comparison. This exceptional strength is 
further validated by strong earnings from memory chipmakers. 
Source: DRAMeXchange, WSTS, MoTIR Korea, BofA Global Research

fund. Back-tested performance depicts the theoretical (not actual) performance of a particular strategy over the time period indicated. 
No representation is being made that any actual portfolio is likely to have achieved returns similar to those shown herein.

performance results are created by applying an investment strategy or methodology to
historical data and attempts to give an indication as to how a strategy might have
performed during a certain period in the past if the product had been in existence during
such time. Back-tested results have inherent limitations including the fact that they are
calculated with the full benefit of hindsight, which allows the security selection
methodology to be adjusted to maximize the returns. Further, the results shown do not

reflect actual trading or the impact that material economic and market factors might
have had on a portfolio manager's decision-making under actual circumstances. Back-
tested returns do not reflect advisory fees, trading costs, or other fees or expenses.

### Page 16

Price objective basis & risk 
Nanya Technology (NNYAF) 
Our PO of NT$800 is derived from 9x 2027-28E P/E, which is c.30% below the long-
term historical average (13x during 2014-25), but near-50% above the previous upturn 
period (6x in 2014-18). Memory stocks usually trade at low valuation multiples during 
peak cycle due to downturn concerns. However, the current DRAM cycle looks 
structurally much better than ever and we expect 2027-28 earnings to exceed 2026 level 
due to the AI growth theme. This could lead to a re-rating (e.g., 9x P/E or higher), with 
solid earnings outlook.

Upside risks: DRAM price increases even after the 1H26 upturn, Big-3 memory 
chipmakers' significant supply cut for commodity DRAM due to HBM, and Nanya Tech's 
more successful ramp-up of DDR4 or DDR5 (better cost or margin improvement).

Downside risks: weaker legacy DDR4 demand (quicker shift to DDR5), and China's more 
successful memory production increase.

potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings ar e: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things,

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
CPO: Optical scale-up is getting closer to reality. A 64-XPU CPO-enabled
compute-rack demonstration highlighted the potential for direct optical links

between accelerators. As 200G/lane electrical channels approach physical
limitations, optical scale-up will become more important, particularly for customized
XPUs. However, pluggagle optics should retain scale-out volume leadership given
advantages in serviceability and cost. Packaging yield, fiber alignment, and reliability
remain key commercialization challenges.
Advanced packaging: Moving beyond 2.5D. MediaTek highlighted custom ASIC
designs approaching 10x reticle size, arguing that 3.5D integration could deliver

approximately 10x better die-to-die energy efficiency versus conventional 2.5D
interconnects. Management expects increasing adoption over the next 3-5 years.
MediaTek also emphasized custom HBM, proprietary die-to-die interconnects, and
DTCO optimization. We see structural opportunities for advanced packaging and
custom ASIC design services as system complexity increases (link).
Memory: Capacity matters alongside bandwidth. SK hynix introduced its MARS

disaggregated memory architecture, demonstrating a 3-5x improvement in time-to-
first-token through shared KV-cache access versus recomputation. Meanwhile, HBF
targets approximately half of HBM bandwidth but 14x higher capacity, with samples
expected in early 2028. Inference workloads will increasingly require heterogeneous
memory architectures encompassing HBM, DRAM, and NAND, potentially expanding
memory content beyond traditional GPU-attached HBM (link).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Optical interconnect: CPO is moving toward rack-scale scale-up, while pluggables
retain a scale-out advantage.
Custom ASICs and packaging: MediaTek sees 3.5D gaining traction as 2.5D
approaches physical limits
Memory architecture: SK hynix targets inference capacity and KV-cache
bottlenecks through MARS arid HBF.
Power and cooling: Racks above 200kW accelerate 800V DC, vertical power

delivery and liquid cooling.

Power and thermal: The next system-level constraints. Demonstrations of 6kw-
class double-sided cold plates vertical power delivery, and advanced microchannel
cooling highlighted intensifying thermal challenges. The transition toward 800V DC
should reduce conversion losses and improve power density. Meanwhile, AMD's
Helion agentic Al rack architecture reinforces incremental CPU demand alongside
GPU infrastructure. Power delivery, advanced cooling, and system integration

capabilities to become increasingly important differentiators.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 164: 5425台半 元富

Source PDF: all report pdf/5425台半 元富.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

台半(5425) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：2027 年產品漲價效益將更全面

一、台半為功率元件製造與封測廠商，車用營收占比最
高 
台半成立於 1979 年並為功率元件製造與封測廠商，晶
圓製造有宜蘭六吋廠和天津四吋廠，後段封測則有天津
和山東兩座廠房，半導體以及條碼印表機(子公司鼎漢
3611 TT, 持股 35.74%)分別占 1H26 營收比重為 38%和
62%，半導體業務主要產品包含 MOSFET、保護元件
TVS 和 ESD、二極體，MOSFET 產品線主要透過和晶
圓代工廠如聯電合作，非 MOSFET 產品線則以自製為
主。若以產品應用而言，車用、工業、3C 和其他分別占
1H26 營收比重為 59%、16%、23%和 2%。因MOSFET
業務拓展有成，營收占比由 2024 年的中個位數提升至
目前 20%，且車用客戶結構由中系擴展至歐美客戶，產
品組合優化為毛利率改善另一主因。 
二、報價逐季調整，2H26 營收獲利可望季季高 
除 7 月調整部分產品價格外，鼎翰併購 Bluebird 綜效顯
現並開始出貨大型企業專案，預估 3Q25 營收 52.35 億
元(+3%QoQ)，受惠於無一次性晶圓報廢影響以及產品
漲價效益，毛利率將由上季之 29.6%提升至 30.8%，營
業利益 5.57 億元(+18%QoQ)，稅後淨利 2.53 億元
(+25%QoQ)，EPS 1.03 元。展望 4Q26，受惠於更多品
項產品將於 11 月漲價，預期 4Q26 營收 53.53 億元
(+2%QoQ)，預估毛利率 31.7%優於前季之 30.8%，營
業利益 6.26 億元(+13%QoQ)，稅後淨利 3 億元
(+19%QoQ)，EPS 1.22 元  
三、去中化轉單效益，以及AI 強勁需求改善產業供需為
營運成長兩大動能 
去年 10 月安世半導體之亂以及今年 4 月揚杰科技被歐
盟列入制裁名單，皆引發全球功率半導體去中化轉單效
應，此兩家業者與台半產品重疊性高，其中安世半導體
轉單效益推估增加單季營收達 3-4 百萬美元，因車用客
戶驗證時程冗長，後續訂單延續性高，至於揚杰科技轉
單效益，經營階層認為尚不顯著。此外，Onsemi 於法
說會上將 AI 資料中心功率半導體市場規模由原先預估
的 120 億美元大幅調升至 500 億美元，雖 AI 營收僅占
公司營收個位數 ， 但AI 外溢需求致使成熟製程產能排擠，
改善功率元件產業供需平衡與價格，且國際 IDM 大廠
將資源轉移至 AI 用高瓦數電源模組與高階 SiC/GaN，
此亦致使車用與工控 MOSFET、二極體供給較過去吃
緊。 
四、產品漲價效益尚未完全反映 
全球功率半導體去中化轉單效應 ， 且今年以來Infineon、
TI、NX、STM 以及士蘭微等多家大廠陸續調漲報價，皆
反映出功率元件市場供需改善，且銅、金價格居高不下
致使成本增加，加計委外晶圓代工和封測成本皆增加，
故致使台半今年 4、7 和 11 月三次調漲報價，但今年產
品漲價效益僅限於產能占比近半之非合約客戶。投顧認
為台半今年毛利率改善幅度來自於稼動率提升勝於產品
漲價因素 ， 然功率元件產業受惠去中化轉單和AI 強勁需
求趨勢不變，加計反應委外晶圓代工和封測上揚成本，
預期 2027 年台半產品漲價效應將擴及部分合約客戶。  
五、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 3.78 元和 5.89
元。 
2)考量目前產品上漲僅限於非合約 ，2027 年產品漲價效
益更加全面，給予買進評等，TP 130 元 (2027 PER x 
22)。

## Document 211: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름 - 기업

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

### Page 3

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  October 2026, No.307

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

4 
−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

5 
and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and 
intangible social infrastructure that promotes investment has been established. 
Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ

giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn).

- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

## Document 258: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-19).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports. 1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.
Exhibit 1: FDI Inflows to the U.S.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

4 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026, 2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

trong thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh 
 Biwase là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực cung cấp nước sạch với 
thị phần đạt 100% tại Bình Dương. 
Ngoài ra, công ty còn mở rộng sang 
lĩnh vực đầu tư nhà máy xử lý nước và 
chất thải rắn nhằm hoàn thiện hệ 
sinh thái dịch vụ hạ tầng môi trường 
tại khu vực. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Nắng nóng thúc đẩy tiêu thụ nước sạch khối dân cư, đem về tổng doanh thu cấp nước tại Bình Dương và 
Bình Phước năm 2026/2027 đạt 2,953/3,192 tỷ VND (6.8%/7.4%). 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Nhà máy nước Nhị Thành giai đoạn 3 đi vào hoạt động giúp tổng doanh thu nhà máy đạt lần lượt 213/247 tỷ 
VND (+16%/16% YoY) trong 2026/2027 và duy trì tăng trưởng trung bình 15% đến 2030. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước: 
tại đây 
BWE hưởng lợi từ khung giá xử lý rác thải được phê duyệt, giúp doanh thu đạt 1,069/1,234 tỷ VND trong 
2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15%

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số 
 Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 
325 tỷ VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ). Mảng cung cấp nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp 
tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% 
QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025. Kinh doanh tăng trưởng 
tích cực là nhờ giá nước đầu vào mua từ TDM thấp hơn giúp tăng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp, cùng với không phát sinh lỗ tỷ giá như cùng kỳ năm ngoái.

### Page 4

4

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh 
được thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng 
nóng hơn; trong khi triển vọng 
nhóm khách hàng công nghiệp bị 
ảnh hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan 
 Dự báo đồng thuận xác suất xảy ra El Nino ở mức xấp xỉ 100% từ 2H2026 kéo 
dài đến tháng đầu 2027 cho thấy cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt 
nắng nóng sẽ gay gắt và kéo dài hơn so với các năm trước. Đây là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY 
(+2.6/1.1 ppts). Trái lại, sau khi section 301 được công bố và có hiệu lực thay 
thế section 122, tình hình thuế quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho 
các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống của Việt Nam. Cụ thể, Section 301 không tự 
hết hạn và có nền tảng pháp lý chắc chắn (ít nhất là trong nhiệm kỳ của 
Trump); đồng thời áp dụng hạn ngạch thuế quan cho một lượng hàng dệt may 
nhất định của một số nước không có Việt Nam, khiến bối cảnh bất lợi cho các 
ngành sản xuất truyền thống. Tuy nhiên, với việc Bình Dương đang tập trung 
đầu tư hạ tầng khu công nghiệp hiện đại nhằm thu hút dòng vốn FDI vào các 
lĩnh vực công nghệ cao, sản lượng nước sạch sẽ được duy trì khi tiêu thụ khối 
sản xuất truyền thống được bù đắp bởi nhóm công nghệ cao. Theo đó, trong 
năm 2026, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng nhẹ dự báo sản lượng nước sạch tại Bình 
Dương & Bình Phước đạt 224.9 triệu m3 (+6.8% YoY), với mức tăng của nhóm 
sản xuất/sinh hoạt/hành chính lần lượt là 5.8%/7.5%/11% YoY.

Biểu đồ 4. Xác suất tăng dần của EL Nino

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV tổng hợp

Mảng cấp nước: Tiến độ điều chỉnh 
giá bán nước gián đoạn do môi 
trường lạm phát leo cao 
 Do tác động của cuộc chiến Mỹ - Iran gây ra gián đoạn dòng chảy vận tải dầu 
tại eo biển Hormuz, giá nhiên liệu trong nước đã tăng mạnh từ đầu năm (Dầu 
Diesel - +64.3%, E5 RON 92 – +40%) và khiến cho tình hình lạm phát trong nước 
diễn biến tiêu cực: CPI bình quân 8 tháng năm 2026 tăng 4.5% với chỉ số giá 
nhóm nhà ở, điện nước, chất đốt và vật liệu xây dựng tăng cao nhất với 6.7% và 
giao thông tăng 5.4%. Trong bối cảnh đó, chúng tôi cho rằng tiến độ điều 
chỉnh giá bán nước tại các thị trường của BWE có khả năng bị đẩy lùi sang 
2027-2028 hoặc cho đến khi tình hình giá nhiên liệu ổn định trở lại. Trong dài 
hạn, khả năng đàm phán giá nước thành công là khá cao bởi: i) Thông tư 
145/2025/TT-BTC có hiệu lực từ 2026 là căn cứ để UBND quy định tỷ suất lợi 
nhuận định mức các dự án nhà máy nước; ii) BWE và cổ đông lớn là TDM dự 
kiến đầu tư và mở rộng công suất cung cấp – phân phối nước tại các vùng hẻo 
lánh nên có khả năng tăng áp lực đàm phán tăng giá nước tại Bình Dương. 
Trong kịch bản thận trọng, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng giá nước sạch trung bình khu
